- **intent-driven-development**: Turn an ambiguous or high-impact change into scoped, verifiable acceptance criteria (observable AC-NNN, explicit scope, named verification methods, and a [revised] protocol that forbids silently dropping a criterion) before or alongside implementation. - **intersect.ja**: AutoCAD の交差(INTERSECT、IN)の使い方。2 つ以上の 3D ソリッド、または同じ平面上のリージョンを、すべてに共通する部分だけにして残りを削除します。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.ja.md))
- **intersect.ko**: AutoCAD 교집합(INTERSECT, IN) 사용법: 둘 이상의 3D 솔리드나 같은 평면의 영역에서 모두가 공유하는 부분만 남기고 나머지는 지웁니다. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.ko.md))
- **intersect.pl**: Jak działa ILOCZYN (INTERSECT) w AutoCADzie: z dwóch lub więcej brył 3D albo regionów w jednej płaszczyźnie zostaje tylko część wspólna, reszta znika. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.pl.md))

- **intersect.ru**: Как работает ПЕРЕСЕЧЕНИЕ (INTERSECT) в AutoCAD: из двух и более 3D-тел или областей в одной плоскости остается только их общая часть, остальное стирается. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.ru.md))
- **intersect.zh-CN**: CAD 交集怎么用：AutoCAD 的 INTERSECT（快捷键 IN）将两个或多个三维实体，或同一平面中的面域，修剪为它们的公共部分，其余部分删除。在浏览器中免费试用。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/intersect.zh-CN.md))

- **interview**: **Not `/prep`.** This is a real interview: on an application in the tracker, or on a process with no application at all, such as a referral that led to a recruiter's call. A recruiter screen is an interview stage, referred or not. `/prep` prepares a networking call from a thread and takes a person's name instead. - **introduction**: A command is a `.md` file under `.claude/commands/` with YAML frontmatter. The frontmatter has a `description`, an optional `argument-hint`, and `allowed-tools`, which limits the tools that the command can use. A command is different from a skill: it never loads automatically. You start it explicitly when you type its name, for example `/dk-review`. ([dotkernel/dotboost](https://github.com/dotkernel/dotboost/blob/HEAD/docs/book/v1/commands/introduction.md) · ★9)

Two-stage procedure — outline pass writes the section plan to _outline.json (operator edit point); section pass renders each section in the order the outline specifies. Report-only — owns NO rubric dimension, all nine scores stay null, and critical_flag is ALWAYS false. NOT an FTO opinion.
